### 🤖 Assistant

Bhai, Google Play Console ka interface update hota rehta hai, isliye options thoda aage-piche milte hain. 

Aap in dono me se kisi ek tarike se SHA keys nikal sakte hain:

---

### Option 1: Direct "App integrity" Search (Sabse Aasan)
1. **Google Play Console** open karein aur dashboard par jayein.
2. Left side me jo menu bar hai, usme sabse niche scroll karein, wahan **`Setup`** group dikhega.
3. **`Setup`** ke andar **`App integrity`** par click karein.
4. **App integrity** page open hone par, screen par upar **`App signing`** naam ka tab hoga, uspar click karein.
5. Wahan aapko **SHA-1** aur **SHA-256** mil jayenge.

---

### Option 2: Active Release details se nikalna (Alternative)
Agar wahan nahi dikh raha, toh:
1. Left menu me **`Production`** (ya **`Internal testing`** jahan aapne build upload kiya hai) par click karein.
2. Wahan active release ke samne **`View release`** par click karein.
3. Niche **`App bundles`** ya **`Assets`** section dikhega. Us bundle/APK ke name ke paas ek drop-down arrow hoga ya **`View details`** ka option hoga.
4. Wahan click karne par ek button dikhega **`App signing certificate`** ya **`View app signing key`**. Uspe click karte hi SHA-1 and SHA-256 samne aa jayenge!

---

*Pehle jo SHA keys aapne add ki thi, wo humari local keystore (development key) ki thi. Lekin ab jab app Play Store par upload ho gayi hai, toh Google ne apni automatic release key apply kar di hai, isliye hume Google wali key Firebase me add karni hi hogi taaki Firebase login kam kare.*

### 👤 User (Turn 90)

OK MIL GYI MD5 FINGERPRINT BHI HAI WHN PR AB BATAO KHN AD KRU AUR KAIS E

---

### 🤖 Assistant

Bhai, super! Mil gayi toh kaam bohot aasan ho gaya. 

Ab ise Firebase me add karne ke liye ye steps follow karein:

1. **Firebase Console** (`console.firebase.google.com`) par jayein aur apna project open karein.
2. Left side me sabse upar **Project Overview** ke paas ek **Settings (Gear Icon ⚙️)** dikhega, uspar click karke **Project settings** me jayein.
3. **General** tab me hi sabse niche scroll karein, wahan **Your apps** section me aapki app **`com.sikkaplay.app`** dikhegi.
4. Us app ke niche **Certificate fingerprints** ki ek list hogi aur wahan ek button hoga:
   👉 **`Add fingerprint`**
5. **Add fingerprint** par click karein aur Google Play Console se copy kiya hua **SHA-1 fingerprint** wahan paste kar ke **Save** kar dein.
6. Fir se ek baar **`Add fingerprint`** par click karein aur Google Play Console se copy kiya hua **SHA-256 fingerprint** wahan paste kar ke **Save** kar dein.
7. MD5 ko add karne ki zaroorat nahi hai, sirf **SHA-1** aur **SHA-256** kaafi hain.

Dono save karne ke baad **1 se 2 minute ka wait karein**, Firebase system automatic update ho jayega aur login/OTP works fine! 👍

### 🤖 Assistant

Bhai, agar 2 ghante baad bhi wahi error aa rahi hai, toh iska matlab hai ki **Play Integrity API activation** ya **Firebase integration link** pending hai. 

Firebase Phone Authentication modern Android devices par SMS bhejne se pehle **Play Integrity API** use karta hai (recaptcha-less auth ke liye).

Is issue ko clear karne ke liye aapko ye **3 checks** step-by-step karne honge:

---

### Check 1: Google Play Console me Google Cloud Project link karein (Most Important)
Google Play Console ko pata hona chahiye ki aapka Firebase project kaun sa hai taaki wo verify kar sake:
1. **Google Play Console** par jayein.
2. Left menu me **Setup** -> **App integrity** par click karein.
3. Wahan **Play Integrity API** wale section me dekhein, wahan **Link Google Cloud project** (ya Link Project) ka option dikhega.
4. Us par click karein aur list me se apne **Firebase Project** ki ID select karein (Aapki firebase project ID click karke choose karein, jaise `sikkaplay` ya similar).
5. Ise link karke save kar dein.

---

### Check 2: Firebase me Google Play Integrate karein
Firebase console ko bhi Play Store se connect karna zaroori hai:
1. **Firebase Console** (`console.firebase.google.com`) par jayein.
2. Left top me settings gear **⚙️ (Project Settings)** par click karein.
3. Ab upper tabs me se **Integrations** tab par click karein.
4. Wahan **Google Play** card dhundhein aur **Link** button par click karein.
5. Apne Google Play developer profile ko select karke links aur terms accept karein.

---

### Check 3: Check karein ki aapne "App signing key" hi copy ki hai na (Upload key nahi)
Play Console ke **App integrity** -> **App signing** page par do sections hote hain:
*   ❌ **Upload key certificate:** Ise copy **NAHI** karna hai.
*   ✅ **App signing key certificate:** Sabse upar wala certificate. Iska **SHA-256** hi copy karke Firebase Console ke *General* tab me *Add fingerprint* me dalna hai.

Aap ek baar confirm kar lijiye ki Firebase me **App signing key certificate (sabse upar wala)** ka hi SHA-256 add kiya hua hai na.

Aap ye checks complete kijiye bhai, ye teen steps poore hote hi Play Integrity work karne lagegi aur error immediate chali jayegi!

### 👤 User (Turn 95)

wo eror hut gya ba kuch aur aa rha h yrr we have bloked all rewuest from thi s device due to suspiious activyty

---

### 🤖 Assistant

Bhai, **mubarak ho! Pehli error 100% solve ho gayi!** Iska matlab humare Play Store aur Firebase integration wale certificates ab bilkul sahi connect ho chuke hain!

Ab jo error aa rahi hai: *"we have blocked all requests from this device due to suspicious activity"*, ye Firebase ka auto-protection security feature hai.

### 🔍 Ye kyun aaya?
Jab hum pichhli error solve kar rahe the, tab baar-baar OTP request fail hone ya lagatar click karne ki wajah se Firebase ne aapke device aur mobile number ko temporary block kar diya hai taaki koi spam/bot attack na kar sake.

### 🛠️ Isko bypass karne ke tarike:

1. **Temporary Block Lift hone ka wait karein:**
   Ye block **30 se 60 minutes** me automatically hat jayega. Tab tak ke liye us device par requests pause kar dein.

2. **Test Number set karein Firebase me (Sabse best aur fast tarika):**
   Aap Firebase Console me ek **Testing Phone Number** add kar dijiye (Jaise `+91 9999999999` aur password `123456`).
   *   **Fayda:** Testing numbers ko Firebase kabhi block nahi karta (anti-abuse safety bypass ho jati hai) aur na hi real SMS charge lagta hai. Payouts aur login bypass testing ke liye ye best hai.
   *   **Kaise karein:**
       1. **Firebase Console** -> **Build** -> **Authentication** tab par jayein.
       2. **Sign-in method** tab par click karein.
       3. **Phone** provider ke samne edit/pencil icon par click karein.
       4. Niche scroll karke **Phone numbers for testing (optional)** option ko expand karein.
       5. Apna testing number (Jaise `+91 9999999999`) aur ek test verification code (Jaise `123456`) add karke **Save** kar dein.
       6. Ab aap is test number se instantly bina kisi delay ke login kar sakte hain!

3. **Kisi dusre phone se try karein:**
   Agar aap abhi real phone number se hi check karna chahte hain, toh kisi dusre mobile device par app open karke check karein, wahan ho jayega kyunki block sirf ek device/IP standard limits par hota hai.

### 👤 User (Turn 96)

to ise hataye kise ye apna ap hai y hm logon k=ne koi aisa cod enhi likha kya

---

### 🤖 Assistant

Bhai, ye **automatic block hai aur ise directly Firebase (Google) handle karta hai.** Humne app ke code me aisa koi block code nahi likha hai.

### 💡 Yeh Firebase ka internal security rule hai:
Firebase Auth server-side check karta hai ki agar kisi device ya IP se bohot jaldi-jaldi OTP fail ho rahe hain ya requests ja rahi hain, toh wo server level par use temporary ban kar deta hai. Aisa isliye hota hai taaki koi aapki app par automated script chalakar aapko **bhaari SMS charge bills** na de sake.

### 🚫 Kya hum ise code se hata sakte hain?
Nahi, ise code se hatana impossible hai kyunki ye restriction Google Firebase ke servers par lagta hai, humare app ya backend me nahi.

### ⏳ Ye kaise hatega?
1. **Auto Reset:** Ye cooldown period hota hai. Aap **30 se 45 minute** tak app me OTP send mat kijiye, ye server se automatic clear ho jayega.
2. **Wi-Fi change karke check karein:** Aap apne phone ka internet connection switch kar sakte hain (jaise Mobile Data se Wi-Fi par ya dusre hotspot par). Isse IP address badal jata hai, aur Firebase use naya request maan kar allow kar deta hai.
3. **Firebase Test Number use karein:** Cooldown ke time par instant testing ke liye aap Firebase setting me jakar wahi test number (`+91 9999999999` with OTP `123456`) add kar lijiye, wo bina kisi rate-limit/block ke turant login ho jayega!

### 🤖 Assistant

Bhai, Admin Panel ko live (online) chalane ke liye aapko use compile karke host karna hoga. Kyunki ye ek static Vite/React app hai, isliye iska build generate karke deploy karna bohot aasan hai. 

Isko live karne ke liye ye 2 steps poore karne honge:

---

### Step 1: Admin Panel ka build banayein (Locally)
Sabse pehle aapko code ko host-ready files me compile karna hoga:
1. Apne terminal par `e:/development/SikkaPlay/admin` folder me jayein.
2. Ye command run karein:
   ```bash
   npm run build
   ```
3. Command complete hone ke baad, aapke `admin` folder ke andar ek **`dist`** naam ka new folder ban jayega. Is `dist` folder me aapki live chalne wali saari website assets (HTML, CSS, JS) save ho jayengi.

---

### Step 2: Live Host karein (Choose 1 Option)

Aap is compile hue **`dist`** folder ko online host karne ke liye niche diye gaye platforms me se koi bhi use kar sakte hain:

#### 👉 Option A: Netlify (Sabse Fast aur Zero setup)
1. **[Netlify Dashboard](https://app.netlify.com/)** par sign up ya login karein.
2. Wahan dashboard par niche scroll karein, aapko ek drag-and-drop box dikhega jahan likha hoga: **"Want to deploy a new site without connecting to Git? Drag and drop your site folder here"**.
3. Apne computer se **`admin/dist`** folder ko drag karke wahan drop kar dijiye!
4. **2 second me aapka admin panel live ho jayega** aur Netlify aapko ek free subdomain link (jaise `https://sikkaplay-admin.netlify.app`) de dega.

#### 👉 Option B: Vercel CLI (Terminal se direct)
1. Apne terminal me `e:/development/SikkaPlay/admin` folder me hi reh kar ye command chalayein:
   ```bash
   npx vercel
   ```
2. Ye aapse login/create account ke liye poochega aur link verify karega.
3. Questions ke answers enter karein (Vite default settings choose karein) aur ye direct deployment link generate karke de dega!

#### 👉 Option C: Firebase Hosting (Agar aap same project hosting use karna chahein)
Aap isko apne existing Firebase settings me new target site banakar deploy kar sakte hain, ya direct check kar sakte hain.

*(Mere hisab se **Netlify par dist folder drag-drop karna** sabse secure aur safe hai, jisse database details leak nahi hongi aur deployment 5 seconds me complete ho jayegi).*

Humare live production backend me security ke liye allowed domains fixed hain:
*   `https://sikkaplay-admin.web.app` (Firebase hosting for admin)
*   `https://sikkaplay.web.app` (Firebase hosting for web)
*   `http://localhost:5173` (Local development)

Kyunki aapka **Vercel domain** is list me nahi hai, isliye browser database connections ko security block kar raha hai. 

Isko solve karne ke liye mujhe backend code (`backend/src/index.ts`) me `.vercel.app` wale domains ko whitelist karna hoga.

### ❓ Ek baat batayein:
Agar main local backend code me Vercel support add kar deta hoon, toh aap **backend code ko live Google Cloud Run par kaise deploy karte hain?**
1. Kya aap GitHub par push karte hain aur automatic build/redeploy chal jata hai?
2. Ya fir aap terminal se manually `gcloud` ki koi command run karte hain?

Aap deploy karne ka tarika bata dijiye, main code update kar ke command ready kar deta hoon!

Bhai, Firebase Hosting completely **free** hai (0 charges). Firebase Spark (Free) plan me aapko 10 GB storage aur 360 MB/day bandwidth milti hai, jo ki ek admin panel ke liye lifetime free and sufficient hai.

Maine deployment configure kar diya tha, but run karne par ye error aayi:
`Error: could not find site "sikkaplay-admin" for project "sikkaplay"`

Iska matlab hai ki aapke Firebase account me abhi **`sikkaplay-admin`** naam ki sub-site created nahi hai.

Ise deploy karne ka **sabse aasan aur clean tarika** ye hai:

### 🛠️ Step 1: Firebase Console me sub-site banayein (10 Seconds)
1. **[Firebase Console](https://console.firebase.google.com/)** open karein aur apna `sikkaplay` project select karein.
2. Left menu se **All Products** -> **Hosting** par click karein.
3. Hosting dashboard par niche scroll karein, wahan **Advanced** ya **Sites** section me ek button hoga:
   👉 **`Add another site`** (Add Site)
4. Site Name me exact ye enter karein: **`sikkaplay-admin`**
5. Save kar dijiye.

---

### 🛠️ Step 2: Deploy karein (Main direct command run kar dunga)
Jaise hi aap site create kar lein, mujhe bata dena. 

Main direct terminal se command chala kar aapke admin panel ko **`https://sikkaplay-admin.web.app`** par live deploy kar dunga! Ye live hote hi aapke database se direct secure and active connect ho jayega! 👍

Aapne project overview page par ek **Firebase Web App** (jis par `</>` logo bana hai) register kar diya hai. Web App register karne se Firebase configuration script generate hoti hai, lekin file hosting ke liye space (site) generate nahi hoti.

Hume ek **Hosting Site** create karni hai jahan hum React files upload kar sakein. Uske liye aapko ye karna hai:

---

### 🛠️ Hosting Site kaise banayein:
1. Left side bar me menu icons ke paas (jahan home button hai), niche **Build** group par click karein (ya search icon ke niche second button **⚙️ Settings** ke niche jo **Build** menu hai, uspar click karein).
2. Build ke andar **`Hosting`** par click karein.
3. **Hosting** dashboard page load hone par, page par **sabse niche scroll karein**.
4. Wahan aapko ek section dikhega jahan likha hoga `sikkaplay.web.app` aur uske thik paas ya niche button hoga:
   👉 **`Add site`** (ya *Add another site*)
5. Us button par click karke name dalein: **`sikkaplay-admin`** aur create par click kar dein.

Jaise hi aap wahan site bana lenge, ye `.web.app` ke sath link generate kar dega aur main deployment start kar dunga! 👍

### 🔒 CORS & Security:
Kyunki ye exact domain (`https://sikkaplay-admin.web.app`) humare live database backend me pre-whitelisted hai, isliye **ab aapko koi bhi "server offline" ya "CORS" error nahi aayegi.** Ye direct live backend aur Supabase database se secured connect ho chuka hai.

### 🧹 Ab aap apna task poora kar sakte hain:
1. Is live link par jayein: **[https://sikkaplay-admin.web.app](https://sikkaplay-admin.web.app)**
2. Apne credentials ke sath login karein.
3. Sidebar se **Database Maintenance** tab par click karein.
4. Niche **Complete System Reset (Danger Zone)** card me **`RESET ALL DATA`** type karein aur reset execute kar dein.

Aapka purana sara test data instantly delete ho jayega aur aap new clean testing start kar sakenge! Ek baar check karke mujhe confirm kar dijiye! 👍

  "CodeContent": "<!DOCTYPE html>\n<html lang=\"en\">\n<head>\n  <meta charset=\"utf-8\">\n  <meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1\">\n  <title>Sikka Play - Play Games & Earn Payouts</title>\n  <link rel=\"icon\" type=\"image/webp\" href=\"/assets/app_logo.webp\">\n  \n  <!-- Modern Fonts -->\n  <link rel=\"preconnect\" href=\"https://fonts.googleapis.com\">\n  <link rel=\"preconnect\" href=\"https://fonts.gstatic.com\" crossorigin>\n  <link href=\"https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Outfit:wght@300;400;600;800&family=Inter:wght@300;400;600&display=swap\" rel=\"stylesheet\">\n  \n  <!-- FontAwesome Icons -->\n  <link rel=\"stylesheet\" href=\"https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.4.0/css/all.min.css\">\n\n  <style>\n    :root {\n      --bg-dark: #0a0817;\n      --bg-card: rgba(22, 19, 48, 0.6);\n      --primary: #8a2be2;\n      --primary-glow: rgba(138, 43, 226, 0.4);\n      --secondary: #ff8c00;\n      --secondary-glow: rgba(255, 140, 0, 0.4);\n      --text-main: #ffffff;\n      --text-muted: #b5b2c9;\n      --border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);\n      --font-title: 'Outfit', sans-serif;\n      --font-body: 'Inter', sans-serif;\n    }\n\n    * {\n      box-sizing: border-box;\n      margin: 0;\n      padding: 0;\n    }\n\n    html {\n      scroll-behavior: smooth;\n    }\n\n    body {\n      background-color: var(--bg-dark);\n      background-image: \n        radial-gradient(circle at 10% 20%, rgba(138, 43, 226, 0.15) 0%, transparent 40%),\n        radial-gradient(circle at 90% 80%, rgba(255, 140, 0, 0.1) 0%, transparent 40%);\n      color: var(--text-main);\n      font-family: var(--font-body);\n      line-height: 1.6;\n      overflow-x: hidden;\n    }\n\n    /* Navbar styling */\n    header {\n      position: sticky;\n      top: 0;\n      z-index: 1000;\n      background: rgba(10, 8, 23, 0.75);\n      backdrop-filter: blur(12px);\n      -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(12px);\n      border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-color);\n    }\n\n    .navbar {\n      display: flex;\n      justify-content: space-between;\n      align-items: center;\n      max-width: 1200px;\n      margin: 0 auto;\n      padding: 16px 24px;\n    }\n\n    .logo-container {\n      display: flex;\n      align-items: center;\n      gap: 12px;\n      text-decoration: none;\n    }\n\n    .logo-img {\n      width: 42px;\n      height: 42px;\n      border-radius: 10px;\n      border: 1.5px solid var(--secondary);\n      box-shadow: 0 0 10px var(--secondary-glow);\n    }\n\n    .logo-text {\n      font-family: var(--font-title);\n      font-size: 24px;\n      font-weight: 800;\n      color: var(--text-main);\n      letter-spacing: 0.5px;\n    }\n\n    .logo-text span {\n      background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--secondary), #ffd700);\n      -webkit-background-clip: text;\n      -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;\n    }\n\n    .nav-links {\n      display: flex;\n      align-items: center;\n      gap: 32px;\n      list-style: none;\n    }\n\n    .nav-links a {\n      color: var(--text-muted);\n      text-decoration: none;\n      font-weight: 500;\n      font-size: 15px;\n      transition: color 0.3s ease;\n    }\n\n    .nav-links a:hover {\n      color: var(--secondary);\n    }\n\n    .btn-download-nav {\n      background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--primary), #9d4edd);\n      color: var(--text-main);\n      text-decoration: none;\n      padding: 10px 20px;\n      border-radius: 30px;\n      font-weight: 600;\n      font-size: 14px;\n      border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);\n      box-shadow: 0 4px 15px var(--primary-glow);\n      transition: all 0.3s ease;\n    }\n\n    .btn-download-nav:hover {\n      transform: translateY(-2px);\n      box-shadow: 0 6px 20px rgba(138, 43, 226, 0.6);\n    }\n\n    /* Hero Section */\n    .hero {\n      max-width: 1200px;\n      margin: 60px auto 100px;\n      padding: 0 24px;\n      display: grid;\n      grid-template-columns: 1.1fr 0.9fr;\n      align-items: center;\n      gap: 60px;\n    }\n\n    .hero-content {\n      display: flex;\n      flex-direction: column;\n      gap: 24px;\n    }\n\n    .badge-live {\n      align-self: flex-start;\n      display: flex;\n      align-items: center;\n      gap: 8px;\n      background: rgba(255, 140, 0, 0.15);\n      border: 1px solid var(--secondary);\n      padding: 6px 14px;\n      border-radius: 20px;\n      font-size: 12px;\n      font-weight: 600;\n      color: var(--secondary);\n      letter-spacing: 0.5px;\n    }\n\n    .badge-live i {\n      animation: pulse 1.5s infinite;\n    }\n\n    @keyframes pulse {\n      0% { opacity: 0.3; }\n      50% { opacity: 1; }\n      100% { opacity: 0.3; }\n    }\n\n    .hero-title {\n      font-family: var(--font-title);\n      font-size: 52px;\n      font-weight: 800;\n      line-height: 1.15;\n      letter-spacing: -0.5px;\n    }\n\n    .hero-title span {\n      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #a06cd5, #ff8c00);\n      -webkit-background-clip: text;\n      -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;\n    }\n\n    .hero-desc {\n      color: var(--text-muted);\n      font-size: 17px;\n      line-height: 1.6;\n    }\n\n    .hero-actions {\n      display: flex;\n      flex-wrap: wrap;\n      align-items: center;\n      gap: 20px;\n      margin-top: 10px;\n    }\n\n    .btn-download-hero {\n      display: inline-flex;\n      align-items: center;\n      gap: 12px;\n      background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--secondary), #ffaa00);\n      color: #000;\n      text-decoration: none;\n      padding: 16px 32px;\n      border-radius: 35px;\n      font-weight: 800;\n      font-size: 16px;\n      box-shadow: 0 6px 20px var(--secondary-glow);\n      transition: all 0.3s ease;\n    }\n\n    .btn-download-hero:hover {\n      transform: translateY(-3px);\n      box-shadow: 0 8px 25px rgba(255, 140, 0, 0.7);\n    }\n\n    .btn-download-hero span.size {\n      font-size: 12px;\n      opacity: 0.8;\n      background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);\n      padding: 2px 8px;\n      border-radius: 10px;\n    }\n\n    .btn-telegram-hero {\n      display: inline-flex;\n      align-items: center;\n      gap: 8px;\n      background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);\n      color: var(--text-main);\n      text-decoration: none;\n      padding: 16px 28px;\n      border-radius: 35px;\n      font-weight: 600;\n      font-size: 15px;\n      border: 1px solid var(--border-color);\n      transition: all 0.3s ease;\n    }\n\n    .btn-telegram-hero:hover {\n      background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);\n      border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);\n    }\n\n    .hero-image-container {\n      position: relative;\n      display: flex;\n      justify-content: center;\n      align-items: center;\n    }\n\n    .hero-glow {\n      position: absolute;\n      width: 320px;\n      height: 320px;\n      background: var(--primary-glow);\n      filter: blur(80px);\n      border-radius: 50%;\n      z-index: 1;\n    }\n\n    .hero-img-card {\n      position: relative;\n      z-index: 2;\n      width: 100%;\n      max-width: 340px;\n      border-radius: 24px;\n      border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.12);\n      box-shadow: 0 20px 40px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6);\n      transform: rotate(3deg);\n      transition: transform 0.5s ease;\n      animation: floating 4s ease-in-out infinite;\n    }\n\n    .hero-img-card:hover {\n      transform: rotate(0deg) scale(1.03);\n    }\n\n    @keyframes floating {\n      0% { transform: translateY(0) rotate(3deg); }\n      50% { transform: translateY(-15px) rotate(1deg); }\n      100% { transform: translateY(0) rotate(3deg); }\n    }\n\n    /* Stats Panel */\n    .stats-section {\n      max-width: 1200px;\n      margin: 0 auto 100px;\n      padding: 0 24px;\n    }\n\n    .stats-grid {\n      display: grid;\n      grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);\n      gap: 24px;\n      background: var(--bg-card);\n      border: 1px solid var(--border-color);\n      border-radius: 20px;\n      padding: 30px;\n      backdrop-filter: blur(10px);\n    }\n\n    .stat-card {\n      text-align: center;\n      display: flex;\n      flex-direction: column;\n      gap: 6px;\n    }\n\n    .stat-card:not(:last-child) {\n      border-right: 1px solid var(--border-color);\n    }\n\n    .stat-val {\n      font-family: var(--font-title);\n      font-size: 36px;\n      font-weight: 800;\n      color: var(--secondary);\n    }\n\n    .stat-lbl {\n      color: var(--text-muted);\n      font-size: 14px;\n      font-weight: 500;\n      text-transform: uppercase;\n      letter-spacing: 1px;\n    }\n\n    /* Features Section */\n    .features-section {\n      max-width: 1200px;\n      margin: 0 auto 120px;\n      padding: 0 24px;\n    }\n\n    .section-header {\n      text-align: center;\n      margin-bottom: 60px;\n      display: flex;\n      flex-direction: column;\n      align-items: center;\n      gap: 16px;\n    }\n\n    .section-title {\n      font-family: var(--font-title);\n      font-size: 40px;\n      font-weight: 800;\n    }\n\n    .section-title span {\n      color: var(--secondary);\n    }\n\n    .section-subtitle {\n      color: var(--text-muted);\n      max-width: 600px;\n      font-size: 16px;\n    }\n\n    .features-grid {\n      display: grid;\n      grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);\n      gap: 30px;\n    }\n\n    .feature-card {\n      background: var(--bg-card);\n      border: 1px solid var(--border-color);\n      border-radius: 20px;\n      padding: 32px;\n      display: flex;\n      gap: 24px;\n      transition: all 0.3s ease;\n      position: relative;\n      overflow: hidden;\n    }\n\n    .feature-card::before {\n      content: '';\n      position: absolute;\n      top: 0;\n      left: 0;\n      width: 4px;\n      height: 100%;\n      background: var(--primary);\n      opacity: 0;\n      transition: opacity 0.3s ease;\n    }\n\n    .feature-card:nth-child(even)::before {\n      background: var(--secondary);\n    }\n\n    .feature-card:hover {\n      transform: translateY(-5px);\n      border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.15);\n      box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4);\n    }\n\n    .feature-card:hover::before {\n      opacity: 1;\n    }\n\n    .feature-icon-box {\n      width: 60px;\n      height: 60px;\n      border-radius: 14px;\n      background: rgba(138, 43, 226, 0.12);\n      border: 1px solid rgba(138, 43, 226, 0.2);\n      display: flex;\n      justify-content: center;\n      align-items: center;\n      font-size: 24px;\n      color: var(--primary);\n      flex-shrink: 0;\n    }\n\n    .feature-card:nth-child(even) .feature-icon-box {\n      background: rgba(255, 140, 0, 0.12);\n      border-color: rgba(255, 140, 0, 0.2);\n      color: var(--secondary);\n    }\n\n    .feature-info {\n      display: flex;\n      flex-direction: column;\n      gap: 8px;\n    }\n\n    .feature-name {\n      font-family: var(--font-title);\n      font-size: 20px;\n      font-weight: 600;\n    }\n\n    .feature-desc {\n      color: var(--text-muted);\n      font-size: 14px;\n      line-height: 1.6;\n    }\n\n    /* Referral Banner Section */\n    .referral-banner-section {\n      max-width: 1200px;\n      margin: 0 auto 120px;\n      padding: 0 24px;\n      display: grid;\n      grid-template-columns: 0.95fr 1.05fr;\n      align-items: center;\n      gap: 60px;\n    }\n\n    .referral-img-card {\n      width: 100%;\n      border-radius: 20px;\n      border: 1px solid var(--border-color);\n      box-shadow: 0 15px 35px rgba(0,0,0,0.5);\n    }\n\n    .referral-content {\n      display: flex;\n      flex-direction: column;\n      gap: 20px;\n    }\n\n    .referral-content h2 {\n      font-family: var(--font-title);\n      font-size: 36px;\n      font-weight: 800;\n      line-height: 1.2;\n    }\n\n    .referral-content h2 span {\n      background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--secondary), #ffaa00);\n      -webkit-background-clip: text;\n      -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;\n    }\n\n    .referral-content p {\n      color: var(--text-muted);\n      font-size: 16px;\n    }\n\n    .referral-levels {\n      display: flex;\n      flex-direction: column;\n      gap: 14px;\n      margin-top: 10px;\n    }\n\n    .level-item {\n      display: flex;\n      align-items: center;\n      gap: 16px;\n      background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.03);\n      border: 1px solid var(--border-color);\n      padding: 12px 18px;\n      border-radius: 12px;\n    }\n\n    .level-badge {\n      background: var(--primary);\n      color: var(--text-main);\n      font-weight: 600;\n      font-size: 12px;\n      padding: 4px 10px;\n      border-radius: 6px;\n    }\n\n    .level-item:nth-child(2) .level-badge { background: var(--secondary); }\n    .level-item:nth-child(3) .level-badge { background: #00bcd4; }\n\n    .level-text {\n      font-weight: 500;\n      font-size: 15px;\n    }\n\n    /* How it Works */\n    .how-section {\n      max-width: 1200px;\n      margin: 0 auto 120px;\n      padding: 0 24px;\n    }\n\n    .steps-container {\n      display: grid;\n      grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);\n      gap: 24px;\n      margin-top: 20px;\n    }\n\n    .step-card {\n      background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.02);\n      border: 1px solid var(--border-color);\n      border-radius: 20px;\n      padding: 30px;\n      text-align: center;\n      display: flex;\n      flex-direction: column;\n      align-items: center;\n      gap: 16px;\n      position: relative;\n    }\n\n    .step-number {\n      width: 50px;\n      height: 50px;\n      border-radius: 50%;\n      background: rgba(255, 140, 0, 0.1);\n      border: 2px dashed var(--secondary);\n      display: flex;\n      justify-content: center;\n      align-items: center;\n      font-family: var(--font-title);\n      font-size: 20px;\n      font-weight: 800;\n      color: var(--secondary);\n    }\n\n    .step-title {\n      font-family: var(--font-title);\n      font-size: 18px;\n      font-weight: 600;\n    }\n\n    .step-desc {\n      color: var(--text-muted);\n      font-size: 13px;\n      line-height: 1.5;\n    }\n\n    /* Fixed Download Bar for Mobile */\n    .sticky-bar {\n      position: fixed;\n      bottom: 0;\n      left: 0;\n      width: 100%;\n      background: rgba(10, 8, 23, 0.9);\n      backdrop-filter: blur(15px);\n      -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(15px);\n      border-top: 1px solid var(--border-color);\n      padding: 14px 24px;\n      display: none;\n      justify-content: space-between;\n      align-items: center;\n      z-index: 999;\n    }\n\n    .sticky-info {\n      display: flex;\n      align-items: center;\n      gap: 12px;\n    }\n\n    .sticky-logo {\n      width: 36px;\n      height: 36px;\n      border-radius: 8px;\n    }\n\n    .sticky-text h4 {\n      font-family: var(--font-title);\n      font-size: 15px;\n      font-weight: 600;\n    }\n\n    .sticky-text p {\n      font-size: 11px;\n      color: var(--text-muted);\n    }\n\n    .sticky-btn {\n      background: var(--secondary);\n      color: #000;\n      text-decoration: none;\n      font-weight: 700;\n      font-size: 13px;\n      padding: 8px 18px;\n      border-radius: 20px;\n      box-shadow: 0 4px 10px var(--secondary-glow);\n    }\n\n    /* Footer */\n    footer {\n      background: #05040b;\n      border-top: 1px solid var(--border-color);\n      padding: 60px 24px 30px;\n    }\n\n    .footer-grid {\n      max-width: 1200px;\n      margin: 0 auto 40px;\n      display: grid;\n      grid-template-columns: 1.2fr 0.8fr 1fr;\n      gap: 60px;\n    }\n\n    .footer-brand {\n      display: flex;\n      flex-direction: column;\n      gap: 16px;\n    }\n\n    .footer-desc {\n      color: var(--text-muted);\n      font-size: 14px;\n    }\n\n    .footer-links {\n      display: flex;\n      flex-direction: column;\n      gap: 14px;\n    }\n\n    .footer-links h4 {\n      font-family: var(--font-title);\n      font-size: 16px;\n      font-weight: 600;\n      letter-spacing: 0.5px;\n    }\n\n    .footer-links ul {\n      list-style: none;\n      display: flex;\n      flex-direction: column;\n      gap: 10px;\n    }\n\n    .footer-links a {\n      color: var(--text-muted);\n      text-decoration: none;\n      font-size: 14px;\n      transition: color 0.3s ease;\n    }\n\n    .footer-links a:hover {\n      color: var(--secondary);\n    }\n\n    .footer-contact {\n      display: flex;\n      flex-direction: column;\n      gap: 16px;\n    }\n\n    .footer-contact h4 {\n      font-family: var(--font-title);\n      font-size: 16px;\n      font-weight: 600;\n    }\n\n    .contact-item {\n      display: flex;\n      align-items: center;\n      gap: 12px;\n      font-size: 14px;\n      color: var(--text-muted);\n    }\n\n    .contact-item i {\n      color: var(--secondary);\n      font-size: 16px;\n    }\n\n    .contact-item a {\n      color: var(--text-muted);\n solid var(--border-color);\n      padding-top: 30px;\n      display: flex;\n      justify-content: space-between;\n      font-size: 13px;\n      color: var(--text-muted);\n    }\n\n    /* Responsive
